    window.loadScript = (url, { charset = 'UTF-8', ...attributes } = {}, location = 'head') => {
      const existingScript = document.querySelector('script[src="' + url + '"]');
      if (existingScript) {
        return;
      }

      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.src = url;
      script.type = 'text/javascript';

      script.async = true;
      script.charset = charset;

      if (attributes) {
        Object.entries(attributes).forEach(([key, value]) => {
          script.setAttribute(key, value);
        });
      }

      if (location === 'head') {
        document.head.appendChild(script);
      } else {
        document.body.appendChild(script);
      }

      return script;
    };

proudly joins the UK government&#39;s Cyber Resilience Pledge&quot;],&quot;slug&quot;:[0,&quot;cloudflare-joins-uk-cyber-resilience-pledge&quot;],&quot;excerpt&quot;:[0,&quot;The pledge is a voluntary framework inviting organizations to commit to foundational cyber security governance, board-level accountability, and supply chain rigor. For over a decade, Cloudflare has pioneered the core pillars of this framework: democratizing security, leadership accountability, and radical transparency.&quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;Today, the UK government launched the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.gov.uk/government/news/businesses-across-britain-sign-up-to-cyber-resilience-pledge-as-ministers-urge-firms-to-strengthen-cyber-defences\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cyber Resilience Pledge&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;: a voluntary framework inviting organizations to commit to foundational cybersecurity governance, board-level accountability, and comprehensive cybersecurity coverage across supply chains. &lt;b&gt;Cloudflare is &lt;/b&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://assets.ctfassets.net/slt3lc6tev37/1WKiJC2ISsPozmIWk30ao5/99ff2bec7c1d9c927d221bf3f4aea218/DSIT_Cyber_Resilience_Pledge___Cloudflare__Letterhead.pdf\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;b&gt;&lt;u&gt;proud to join&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;b&gt; the pledgeâs founding cohort of signatories &lt;/b&gt;and continue our long-standing work with the Department of Science, Innovation and Technology (DSIT), National Cyber Security Centre, and others to shape a more secure, future-ready digital economy for the UK&lt;b&gt;.&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/cyber-resilience-pledge/cyber-resilience-pledge-declaration\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;The pledge&amp;#39;s&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; core pillars â democratizing security, leadership accountability, and radical transparency â have been at the heart of Cloudflare since day one. Instead of approaching this framework as a new set of commitments to meet, we see it as a welcome validation from the UK government of the security philosophy and principles Cloudflare has championed for over a decade. We are glad to see the rest of the industry moving in this direction.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This pledge is an important step, and it comes at a time of significant cyber risk. In the first quarter of 2026, Cloudflare&amp;#39;s global network blocked an average of &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/press/press-releases/2026/cloudflare-2026-threat-intelligence-report-nation-state-actors-and/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;234 billion cyber threats every day&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Recently, we mitigated a hyper-volumetric DDoS attack that peaked at &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/ddos-threat-report-2025-q4/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;31.4 Tbps&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. At the end of 2025, Cloudflare data showed that the UK had risen to be the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/ddos-threat-report-2025-q4/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;sixth-most targeted&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; location across the globe for DDoS attacks, with threat actors increasingly targeting application-layer services in financial services, aviation, and regional government infrastructure. This trend is consistent with broader data from the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.gov.uk/government/statistics/cyber-security-breaches-survey-20252026/cyber-security-breaches-survey-20252026#:~:text=Just%20over%20four%20in%20ten,UK%20charities%20%5Bfootnote%201%5D.\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;UK Cyber Security Breaches Survey&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, which revealed that 43% of surveyed British businesses and 28% of charities reported suffering from a cyber incident this past year.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;At the same time, frontier AI models are rapidly changing the security landscape, lowering the barrier to entry for attackers, and enabling more automated vulnerability scanning and more convincing phishing campaigns. Cloudflare has long been preparing for this shift. The defensive architecture we recently &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/frontier-model-defense/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;published&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; for frontier cyber models reflects the same principle: security has to evolve as quickly as the threats companies face. Every layer of that harness architecture, from ML-based attack scoring to Zero Trust access controls, is available to Cloudflare customers today.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Against that backdrop, the pledge does something essential: it recognizes that collective defense is critical. It asks organizations to make cyber resilience a leadership-level priority, to implement appropriate controls to boost threat awareness, and to help ensure supply chains meet a meaningful security baseline. Most breaches still exploit well-understood gaps, like unpatched systems, weak access controls, or poor vendor oversight. Encouraging more organizations to close those gaps through enhanced governance, monitoring, and implementation is a necessary starting point.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Cloudflare is fully aligned with the UK government&amp;#39;s mission to elevate cybersecurity governance within companies and organizations of all sizes. Every organization that raises its baseline makes the Internet safer for everyone else. Our mission at Cloudflare is to help build a better Internet, and we have always believed that cybersecurity and resilience work best when they are universal. A more resilient Internet is a better Internet.Â &lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;why-resilience-matters\&quot;&gt;Why resilience matters&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#why-resilience-matters\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/learning/security/what-is-cyber-resilience/#:~:text=Unlike%20organizations%20that%20focus%20on,can%20cause%20when%20they%20happen.\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cyber resilience&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; is increasingly recognized as a core business requirement. Customers expect services to be available at all times, responsive, and trustworthy. And thatâs true even when the environment gets more challenging to operate in, whether from increased attacks, outages, abuse, or complexity.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Resilience ultimately is not just about recovering after something goes wrong. It is about designing security systems and operating models that can proactively track threat signals, seamlessly absorb disruptions, and adapt to be better. In this way, security and resilience are inseparable. Security controls are what make resilience real.Â &lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;how-cloudflare-helps-strengthen-resilience-through-security\&quot;&gt;How Cloudflare helps strengthen resilience through security&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#how-cloudflare-helps-strengthen-resilience-through-security\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Thanks to the scale of our network, we can help organizations build resilience by shifting protection closer to the edge, before threats reach core systems. We were the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/introducing-universal-ssl/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;first&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to offer SSL certificates, required for traffic encryption, to all users. We protect vulnerable voices through our Impact programs like &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/galileo/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Project Galileo&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; and the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/athenian/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Athenian Project&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. We continuously push the boundaries of Internet cryptography, including the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/post-quantum-crypto-should-be-free/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;deployment of post-quantum cryptography&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; across our network. Our &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/en-gb/plans/free/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;free plan&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; includes unmetered DDoS protection regardless of the size, duration, or volume of attacks, and also provides access to a global content delivery network (CDN) and DNSSEC. These capabilities have historically required expensive hardware and specialist security teams. But the pledgeâs aim of elevating organizational resilience and raising the cyber resilience floor across the UK economy only works if small businesses, local authorities, public services, and startups can afford to participate. Our model directly supports that goal.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;the-network-is-the-sensor\&quot;&gt;The network is the sensor&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-network-is-the-sensor\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Because Cloudflare directly peers with more than 13,000 networks globally, we see attack patterns as they emerge. Threat intelligence collected in one part of the network can be turned into protection everywhere else in a matter of seconds. A threat detected while mitigating an attack on a customer in Singapore can become a rule that helps protect a customer in Sheffield moments later. That same visibility also helps improve how we detect, score, and respond to attacks across Cloudflareâs network and security services. Visibility at scale leads to resilience at scale for Cloudflareâs customers and network.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;cloudflare-is-customer-zero\&quot;&gt;Cloudflare is customer zero&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#cloudflare-is-customer-zero\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Our customers benefit from the exact same industry-leading security products and infrastructure that safeguard our own systems. We test every security layer on ourselves first, and use our own internal learnings to build better security solutions for ourselves and our network. When &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/how-cloudflare-mitigated-yet-another-okta-compromise/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;security incidents&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; or zero-day vulnerabilities emerge, we publish deep-dive technical postmortems on the Cloudflare Blog. We share indicators of compromise and architectural retrospectives, so the broader security community can learn from our telemetry. But transparency is only the first step. We treat every incident as a mandate to make our network more resilient. After a significant outage last fall, our &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/code-orange-fail-small-complete/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Code Orange&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; effort mobilized engineering teams to rebuild for resilience. We require our supply chain to meet comprehensive international standards that incorporate and build upon the core requirements of Cyber Essentials. Cloudflare manages vendor risk globally, prioritizing comprehensive international security frameworks that encompass and exceed the fundamental technical controls of the Cyber Essentials program.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;More specifically, Cloudflare requires critical suppliers to adhere to rigorous, internationally recognized security compliance certifications and reports â primarily ISO 27001 and SOC 2 Type II. These frameworks explicitly require the implementation of firewalls, secure configurations, user access controls, malware protection, and patch management (the five core pillars of Cyber Essentials).&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Cloudflare will continue to use a risk-based methodology to evaluate suppliers. We commend DSIT for expanding access to the Cyber Essentials Supplier Check Tool, which Cloudflare can adopt for localized supply chain validation within the UK. And for global suppliers where UK Cyber Essentials is not a native or practical certification, Cloudflare will accept equivalent international certifications (like ISO 27001) as sufficient verification of a robust security posture. These practices help ensure that Cloudflare&amp;#39;s critical supply chain undergoes stringent security vetting, meeting the risk-reduction outcomes intended by Cyber Essentials.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;onward\&quot;&gt;Onward&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#onward\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Cyber resilience is not a one-time pledge â it is a continuous practice of building systems that fail safely, recover quickly, and learn to be better. For organizations across the UK, it means making cybersecurity a business-critical priority, with leadership buy-in, teams that understand the threats they face, and supply chains managed for risk. the UK government&#39;s Cyber Resilience Pledge&quot;],&quot;slug&quot;:[0,&quot;cloudflare-joins-uk-cyber-resilience-pledge&quot;],&quot;excerpt&quot;:[0,&quot;The pledge is a voluntary framework For over a decade, Cloudflare has pioneered the core pillars of this framework: democratizing security, leadership accountability, and radical transparency.&quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;Today, the UK government launched the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.gov.uk/government/news/businesses-across-britain-sign-up-to-cyber-resilience-pledge-as-ministers-urge-firms-to-strengthen-cyber-defences\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cyber Resilience Pledge&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;: a voluntary framework inviting organizations to commit to foundational cybersecurity governance, board-level accountability, and comprehensive cybersecurity coverage across supply chains. &lt;b&gt;Cloudflare is &lt;/b&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://assets.ctfassets.net/slt3lc6tev37/1WKiJC2ISsPozmIWk30ao5/99ff2bec7c1d9c927d221bf3f4aea218/DSIT_Cyber_Resilience_Pledge___Cloudflare__Letterhead.pdf\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;b&gt;&lt;u&gt;proud to join&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;b&gt; the pledgeâs founding cohort of signatories &lt;/b&gt;and continue our long-standing work with the Department of Science, Innovation and Technology (DSIT), National Cyber Security Centre, and others to shape a more secure, future-ready digital economy for the UK&lt;b&gt;.&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/cyber-resilience-pledge/cyber-resilience-pledge-declaration\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;The pledge&amp;#39;s&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; core pillars â democratizing security, leadership accountability, and radical transparency â have been at the heart of Cloudflare since day one. Instead of approaching this framework as a new set of commitments to meet, we see it as a welcome validation from the UK government of the security philosophy and principles Cloudflare has championed for over a decade. We are glad to see the rest of the industry moving in this direction.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This pledge is an important step, and it comes at a time of significant cyber risk. In the first quarter of 2026, Cloudflare&amp;#39;s global network blocked an average of &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/press/press-releases/2026/cloudflare-2026-threat-intelligence-report-nation-state-actors-and/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;234 billion cyber threats every day&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Recently, we mitigated a hyper-volumetric DDoS attack that peaked at &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/ddos-threat-report-2025-q4/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;31.4 Tbps&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. At the end of 2025, Cloudflare data showed that the UK had risen to be the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/ddos-threat-report-2025-q4/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;sixth-most targeted&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; location across the globe for DDoS attacks, with threat actors increasingly targeting application-layer services in financial services, aviation, and regional government infrastructure. This trend is consistent with broader data from the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.gov.uk/government/statistics/cyber-security-breaches-survey-20252026/cyber-security-breaches-survey-20252026#:~:text=Just%20over%20four%20in%20ten,UK%20charities%20%5Bfootnote%201%5D.\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;UK Cyber Security Breaches Survey&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, which revealed that 43% of surveyed British businesses and 28% of charities reported suffering from a cyber incident this past year.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;At the same time, frontier AI models are rapidly changing the security landscape, lowering the barrier to entry for attackers, and enabling more automated vulnerability scanning and more convincing phishing campaigns. Cloudflare has long been preparing for this shift. The defensive architecture we recently &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/frontier-model-defense/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;published&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; for frontier cyber models reflects the same principle: security has to evolve as quickly as the threats companies face. Every layer of that harness architecture, from ML-based attack scoring to Zero Trust access controls, is available to Cloudflare customers today.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Against that backdrop, the pledge does something essential: it recognizes that collective defense is critical. It asks organizations to make cyber resilience a leadership-level priority, to implement appropriate controls to boost threat awareness, and to help ensure supply chains meet a meaningful security baseline. Most breaches still exploit well-understood gaps, like unpatched systems, weak access controls, or poor vendor oversight. Encouraging more organizations to close those gaps through enhanced governance, monitoring, and implementation is a necessary starting point.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Cloudflare is fully aligned with the UK government&amp;#39;s mission to elevate cybersecurity governance within companies and organizations of all sizes. Every organization that raises its baseline makes the Internet safer for everyone else. Our mission at Cloudflare is to help build a better Internet, and we have always believed that cybersecurity and resilience work best when they are universal. A more resilient Internet is a better Internet.Â &lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;why-resilience-matters\&quot;&gt;Why resilience matters&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#why-resilience-matters\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/learning/security/what-is-cyber-resilience/#:~:text=Unlike%20organizations%20that%20focus%20on,can%20cause%20when%20they%20happen.\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cyber resilience&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; is increasingly recognized as a core business requirement. Customers expect services to be available at all times, responsive, and trustworthy. And thatâs true even when the environment gets more challenging to operate in, whether from increased attacks, outages, abuse, or complexity.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Resilience ultimately is not just about recovering after something goes wrong. It is about designing security systems and operating models that can proactively track threat signals, seamlessly absorb disruptions, and adapt to be better. In this way, security and resilience are inseparable. Security controls are what make resilience real.Â &lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;how-cloudflare-helps-strengthen-resilience-through-security\&quot;&gt;How Cloudflare helps strengthen resilience through security&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#how-cloudflare-helps-strengthen-resilience-through-security\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Thanks to the scale of our network, we can help organizations build resilience by shifting protection closer to the edge, before threats reach core systems. We were the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/introducing-universal-ssl/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;first&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to offer SSL certificates, required for traffic encryption, to all users. We protect vulnerable voices through our Impact programs like &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/galileo/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Project Galileo&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; and the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/athenian/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Athenian Project&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. We continuously push the boundaries of Internet cryptography, including the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/post-quantum-crypto-should-be-free/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;deployment of post-quantum cryptography&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; across our network. Our &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/en-gb/plans/free/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;free plan&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; includes unmetered DDoS protection regardless of the size, duration, or volume of attacks, and also provides access to a global content delivery network (CDN) and DNSSEC. These capabilities have historically required expensive hardware and specialist security teams. But the pledgeâs aim of elevating organizational resilience and raising the cyber resilience floor across the UK economy only works if small businesses, local authorities, public services, and startups can afford to participate. Our model directly supports that goal.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;the-network-is-the-sensor\&quot;&gt;The network is the sensor&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-network-is-the-sensor\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Because Cloudflare directly peers with more than 13,000 networks globally, we see attack patterns as they emerge. Threat intelligence collected in one part of the network can be turned into protection everywhere else in a matter of seconds. A threat detected while mitigating an attack on a customer in Singapore can become a rule that helps protect a customer in Sheffield moments later. That same visibility also helps improve how we detect, score, and respond to attacks across Cloudflareâs network and security services. Visibility at scale leads to resilience at scale for Cloudflareâs customers and network.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;cloudflare-is-customer-zero\&quot;&gt;Cloudflare is customer zero&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#cloudflare-is-customer-zero\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Our customers benefit from the exact same industry-leading security products and infrastructure that safeguard our own systems. We test every security layer on ourselves first, and use our own internal learnings to build better security solutions for ourselves and our network. When &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/how-cloudflare-mitigated-yet-another-okta-compromise/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;security incidents&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; or zero-day vulnerabilities emerge, we publish deep-dive technical postmortems on the Cloudflare Blog. We share indicators of compromise and architectural retrospectives, so the broader security community can learn from our telemetry. But transparency is only the first step. We treat every incident as a mandate to make our network more resilient. After a significant outage last fall, our &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/code-orange-fail-small-complete/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Code Orange&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; effort mobilized engineering teams to rebuild for resilience. We require our supply chain to meet comprehensive international standards that incorporate and build upon the core requirements of Cyber Essentials. Cloudflare manages vendor risk globally, prioritizing comprehensive international security frameworks that encompass and exceed the fundamental technical controls of the Cyber Essentials program.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;More specifically, Cloudflare requires critical suppliers to adhere to rigorous, internationally recognized security compliance certifications and reports â primarily ISO 27001 and SOC 2 Type II. These frameworks explicitly require the implementation of firewalls, secure configurations, user access controls, malware protection, and patch management (the five core pillars of Cyber Essentials).&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Cloudflare will continue to use a risk-based methodology to evaluate suppliers. We commend DSIT for expanding access to the Cyber Essentials Supplier Check Tool, which Cloudflare can adopt for localized supply chain validation within the UK. And for global suppliers where UK Cyber Essentials is not a native or practical certification, Cloudflare will accept equivalent international certifications (like ISO 27001) as sufficient verification of a robust security posture. These practices help ensure that Cloudflare&amp;#39;s critical supply chain undergoes stringent security vetting, meeting the risk-reduction outcomes intended by Cyber Essentials.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;onward\&quot;&gt;Onward&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#onward\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Cyber resilience is not a one-time pledge â it is a continuous practice of building systems that fail safely, recover quickly, and learn to be better. For organizations across the UK, it means making cybersecurity a business-critical priority, with leadership buy-in, teams that understand the threats they face, and supply chains managed for risk. The pledge sets a baseline that every organization should strive to meet.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Cloudflare built its platform on the belief that security and resilience should be universal and available to both the smallest developer and the largest enterprise. We take a closer look at all of them, and argue that while they are in the works and show great potential, we should use ML-DSA for now â the best one currently available. &quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;RSA and ECC, cryptographic algorithms that weâve all relied on for decades, are &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/the-quantum-menace/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;vulnerable&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to the attack of sufficiently advanced quantum computers. Such quantum computers do not exist yet, but they seem to be coming &lt;a href=\&quot;https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=9718\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;sooner&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; than expected. Luckily, the solution is already available: migrate to ML-KEM encryption and ML-DSA signatures, which are designed to be resistant to quantum attack. They were standardized &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/nists-first-post-quantum-standards/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;in 2024&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; by the U.S. National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) after an eight-year open international competition.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The migration to post-quantum cryptography is in full swing now. At the time of writing, the majority of traffic handled by Cloudflare is already using ML-KEM encryption, and is thus secured against the threat to data posed by &lt;a href=\&quot;https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harvest_now,_decrypt_later\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;harvest-now-decrypt-later&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; attacks. But encryption is only one part of the equation: to be fully secure against quantum computers capable of breaking classical cryptography, we aim to deploy post-quantum signatures to protect authentication systems from unauthorized access. We are &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/post-quantum-roadmap\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;targeting 2029&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; for Cloudflare to be fully post-quantum secure.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;ML-DSA, the best all-around post-quantum signature scheme standardized today, has its downsides: itâs much larger on the wire, and many &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/fancy-cryptography/fancy-cryptography\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;tricks&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; we were able to perform with RSA and ECC simply cannot be done with ML-DSA. There are better post-quantum signature schemes on the horizon: last month, NIST &lt;a href=\&quot;https://groups.google.com/a/list.nist.gov/g/pqc-forum/c/LXoTAe5AN78/m/ZXgBCNlgDAAJ\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;announced&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; that it is advancing nine post-quantum signature schemes to the third round of the â&lt;a href=\&quot;https://csrc.nist.gov/projects/pqc-dig-sig\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;signatures on-ramp&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;â. And a draft standard for &lt;a href=\&quot;https://falcon-sign.info/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;FN-DSA&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; (nÃ©e Falcon), which was picked from the previous competition, is expected imminently.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;We have been very interested in advances in post-quantum signature algorithms, and wrote about the progress in &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/sizing-up-post-quantum-signatures/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2021&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/nist-post-quantum-surprise/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2022&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/another-look-at-pq-signatures/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2024&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, and &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/pq-2025/#signatures-on-the-horizon\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2025&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. In this blog post weâll treat you to the latest developments in great detail.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;But first we have to deal with the elephant in the room: These new signature algorithms will not be ready in time for the PQ transition â not even close, as we &lt;a href=\&quot;#timelines\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;will see later on&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. The problem is arriving too soon for us to wait. ML-DSA is available today, and it will have to do for the first migration. As Eric Rescorla &lt;a href=\&quot;https://educatedguesswork.org/posts/pq-emergency/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;wrote&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; in 2024:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;blockquote&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;i&gt;You go to war with the algorithms you have, not the ones you wish you had.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/blockquote&gt;&lt;p&gt;Nonetheless, the search for better post-quantum signature algorithms is crucial for several reasons, and we firmly believe it is still the best use of NISTâs limited resources.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Letâs have a look at the signature algorithms in detail. After that weâll look at the timeline for their availability, and the reasons why we still need them.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;the-signature-algorithms\&quot;&gt;The signature algorithms&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-signature-algorithms\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;In the table below, we compare the candidate signature algorithms that progressed to the third round (marked by ð¤), with classical algorithms vulnerable to quantum attack (marked by â), and the post-quantum algorithms that are already standardized ( â) or soon will be (ð).
